# Known answers for tiny-tracker 0.4.0

Generated by `expected/generate.mjs` from running each build; do not edit by hand. Each cell is the set of answers a correct harness may give.
Contract answers are the lost-response driver's own verdict strings at 92bf1ba; LRT-A3 is self-attested until the driver compares recovered digests.
Not detected in 0.4.0: add-label-replaces-list, bulk-misreports-partial, bulk-without-transaction, close-before-confirm, coerce-types, comment-id-dropped, comment-marked-read-only, conflict-as-generic-error, convert-numeric-strings, create-marked-idempotent, delete-refetches-version, drop-unknown-args, force-by-default, ignore-unknown-args-with-notice, in-progress-reported-not-applied, key-store-in-memory, label-by-position, missing-annotations, no-idempotency-key, read-merge-put, read-only-by-annotation, read-only-strict-true, receipt-missing-version, retry-without-key, set-labels-non-destructive, settle-miss-reported-not-applied, shape-claims-applied, shape-missing-failed, shape-text-says-all-saved, shape-text-says-failed, shape-unknown-as-failed, shape-wrong-version, skip-limits, token-in-error, undeclared-title-limit, unknown-denied-tools-ignored, unknown-tools-ignored, update-adds-defaults, vague-validation-error give exactly the correct build's answers on their own rows here. They are MCP-layer defects of the tool surface (what a tool says, accepts or does), which the MWS requirements cover and mcp-writecheck checks; these known answers hold only fx-check's FX- properties and the lost-response contract driver, and none of these rows has a contract row (in-progress-reported-not-applied and settle-miss-reported-not-applied trip MWS-RES-1 and MWS-RES-2). A "held" on these rows does not mean the defect is absent.
